  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "A quartet with its ordered current entries. Here $A, B$ are fixed local units, unrelated to the summand sets, and $d − e = y$. The shared entry $p_{*}$ is reconstructed and varies according to [eq:tree-p-square]. Holding $M_{2}$ or $M_{4}$ uses a residual entry $A$ or $B$; holding $M_{1}$ or $M_{3}$ uses the reconstructed entry $p_{*}$.",
      "figure_id": "fig:tree-quartet",
      "pdf_page": 50,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:tree-quartet",
          "exact_quote": "A quartet with its ordered current entries.  Here $A,B$ are\n fixed local units, unrelated to the summand sets, and $d-e=y$.\n The shared entry $p_*$ is reconstructed and varies according to\n \\eqref{eq:tree-p-square}.  Holding $M_2$\n or $M_4$",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}\n  \\node (n) at (0,0) {$y;\\ (A,B)$};\n  \\node (l) at (-3,-1.35) {$d;\\ (p_*,A)$};\n  \\node (r) at (3,-1.35) {$e;\\ (p_*,B)$};\n  \\node[align=center] (ll) at (-4.8,-2.8)\n        {$M_1$\\\\$p_*C_1M_1$};\n  \\node[align=center] (lr) at (-1.6,-2.8)\n        {$M_2$\\\\$AC_2M_2$};\n  \\node[align=center] (rl) at (1.6,-2.8)\n        {$M_3$\\\\$p_*C_3M_3$};\n  \\node[align=center] (rr) at (4.8,-2.8)\n        {$M_4$\\\\$BC_4M_4$};\n  \\draw (n)--(l) (n)--(r) (l)--(ll) (l)--(lr)\n        (r)--(rl) (r)--(rr);\n \\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A rooted binary tree diagram with three levels:\n- Root node: labeled \"$y; (A, B)$\".\n- Two branches descend to intermediate nodes:\n  - Left branch to child labeled \"$d; (p_*, A)$\".\n  - Right branch to child labeled \"$e; (p_*, B)$\".\n- From each intermediate node, two branches descend to leaf nodes:\n  - From \"$d; (p_*, A)$\": left leaf \"$M_1$\" with label \"$p_* C_1 M_1$\" directly underneath; right leaf \"$M_2$\" with label \"$A C_2 M_2$\" directly underneath.\n  - From \"$e; (p_*, B)$\": left leaf \"$M_3$\" with label \"$p_* C_3 M_3$\" directly underneath; right leaf \"$M_4$\" with label \"$B C_4 M_4$\" directly underneath."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The coefficient recursion for $A_{j}(s)$: multiply the node factor by the two displayed child factors, with no prime-specific transform attached to the integer pivot $p$. Subsequent internal samples in the two descendants are independent in their underlying priors, before the complete-history support indicators are applied.",
      "figure_id": "fig:transfer-recursion",
      "pdf_page": 61,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:transfer-recursion",
          "exact_quote": "The coefficient recursion for $A_j(s)$:\n    multiply the node factor by the two displayed child factors,\n    with no prime-specific transform attached to the integer pivot $p$.\n    Subsequent internal samples in the two descendants are inde",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n    every node/.style={font=\\small,align=center},\n    transfer box/.style={\n      draw=black!65,rounded corners=2pt,\n      inner xsep=6pt,inner ysep=5pt\n    },\n    transfer arrow/.style={-{Latex[length=2mm]},line width=.6pt}\n  ]\n    \\node[transfer box,fill=black!3,text width=14.4cm] (current) {\n      Current state $\\mathcal I_j=(H_+,H_-)$, root frequency $s$\\\\[2pt]\n      Sum over $0<|v|,|w|\\le V_{j-1}$;\n      sample one type-$j$ vector $\\mathbf u$.\\\\[3pt]\n      $\\displaystyle p=\\frac{vH_- - wH_+}{su},\n        \\qquad u=\\prod_a u_a$\\\\[3pt]\n      Node factor, used once: $u\\varphi(\\log p-G)$\n    };\n\n    \\node[transfer box,text width=6.55cm,anchor=north]\n      (left) at ($(current.south)+(-3.75cm,-.65cm)$) {\n      Left child $(p,\\mathbf u,H_+)$\\\\[3pt]\n      $A_{j-1}(v;\\mathbf d,p,\\mathbf u,H_+)$\n    };\n    \\node[transfer box,text width=6.55cm,anchor=north]\n      (right) at ($(current.south)+(3.75cm,-.65cm)$) {\n      Right child $(p,\\mathbf u,H_-)$\\\\[3pt]\n      $\\overline{A_{j-1}(w;\\mathbf d,p,\\mathbf u,H_-)}$\n    };\n\n    \\coordinate (split) at ($(current.south)+(0,-.25cm)$);\n    \\draw[line width=.6pt] (current.south) -- (split);\n    \\draw[transfer arrow] (split) -| (left.north);\n    \\draw[transfer arrow] (split) -| (right.north);\n\n    \\node[anchor=north,text width=14.8cm,inner sep=0pt]\n      at ($(left.south)!.5!(right.south)+(0,-.28cm)$) {\n      The same $p$, $\\mathbf u$, and outside spectator list $\\mathbf d$\n      are used in both children.\\\\[2pt]\n      Retain only $p\\in\\mathbb Z_{>0}$;\n      all support zero conventions of Subsection~\\ref{subsec:coefficient-support} apply.\n    };\n  \\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A recursion diagram enclosed in an outer rounded rectangle:\n- Top block:\n  - Line 1: \"Current state $\\mathcal{I}_j = (H_+, H_-)$, root frequency $s$\"\n  - Line 2: \"Sum over $0 < |v|, |w| \\le V_{j-1}$; sample one type-$j$ vector $\\mathbf{u}$.\"\n  - Centered equations: \"$p = \\frac{vH_- - wH_+}{su}, \\qquad u = \\prod_a u_a$\"\n  - Line 4: \"Node factor, used once: $u\\varphi(\\log p - G)$\"\n- Two downward directed arrows lead from the top block to two child boxes:\n  - Left child box: labeled \"Left child $(p, \\mathbf{u}, H_+)$\" on top, and \"$\\mathcal{A}_{j-1}(v; \\mathbf{d}, p, \\mathbf{u}, H_+)$\" below.\n  - Right child box: labeled \"Right child $(p, \\mathbf{u}, H_-)$\" on top, and \"$\\mathcal{A}_{j-1}(w; \\mathbf{d}, p, \\mathbf{u}, H_-)$\" below.\n- Text below the child boxes:\n  - \"The same $p$, $\\mathbf{u}$, and outside spectator list $\\mathbf{d}$ are used in both children.\"\n  - \"Retain only $p \\in \\mathbb{Z}_{>0}$; all support zero conventions of Subsection 7.4 apply.\""
    }
  ],
